So (a) O112's timing shares (SNAP 0.008 of band years before year 12, HYB 0.816) and PAUSE-S128's LOC and FIRST describe the post-allowance part only [T:overclaim if quoted as the hold]; (b) 'S370 rarely pauses' (O83), the one 'for' of LOSS-TABLES in the 6 Oct 01:52 receipt, is false under a count that includes the allowance-only draw (grade B); (c) on S370 the saved paths show the same signature as S130 (HYB rushes at access 0.095 against PCLSI 0.062, then holds at 0.187 to 0.225 spending its other pots 189k to 62k while PCLSI keeps about 110k; P-LO holds at 0.714 to 0.739 in years 17-18, other pots 123k to 38k).
